FILE: combine_context.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | from __future__ import annotations
     2 | 
     3 | import argparse
     4 | from pathlib import Path
     5 | 
     6 | GENERATED_DIRECTORIES = {
     7 |     ".venv",
     8 |     "__pycache__",
     9 |     "dist",
    10 |     "node_modules",
    11 |     "venv",
    12 | }
    13 | ALWAYS_EXCLUDED_DIRECTORIES = GENERATED_DIRECTORIES | {".git"}
    14 | 
    15 | 
    16 | def is_binary(path: Path) -> bool:
    17 |     with path.open("rb") as file:
    18 |         sample = file.read(8192)
    19 | 
    20 |     if b"\0" in sample:
    21 |         return True
    22 | 
    23 |     text = sample.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
    24 |     control_characters = sum(
    25 |         ord(character) < 32 and character not in "\n\r\t\f\b"
    26 |         for character in text
    27 |     )
    28 |     return len(text) > 0 and control_characters / len(text) > 0.30
    29 | 
    30 | 
    31 | def collect_files(root: Path, output: Path, include_generated: bool) -> list[Path]:
    32 |     excluded_directories = (
    33 |         {".git"} if include_generated else ALWAYS_EXCLUDED_DIRECTORIES
    34 |     )
    35 |     files: list[Path] = []
    36 | 
    37 |     for path in root.rglob("*"):
    38 |         if not path.is_file():
    39 |             continue
    40 | 
    41 |         relative_path = path.relative_to(root)
    42 |         if path.resolve() == output.resolve():
    43 |             continue
    44 |         if excluded_directories.intersection(relative_path.parts):
    45 |             continue
    46 | 
    47 |         files.append(path)
    48 | 
    49 |     return sorted(files, key=lambda path: path.relative_to(root).as_posix().lower())
    50 | 
    51 | 
    52 | def combine_files(root: Path, output: Path, include_generated: bool) -> tuple[int, int]:
    53 |     files = collect_files(root, output, include_generated)
    54 |     binary_files = 0
    55 | 
    56 |     with output.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as context_file:
    57 |         context_file.write("AI PROJECT CONTEXT\n")
    58 |         context_file.write(f"Source directory: {root.resolve()}\n")
    59 |         context_file.write(f"Files included: {len(files)}\n\n")
    60 | 
    61 |         for path in files:
    62 |             relative_path = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
    63 |             context_file.write("=" * 80)
    64 |             context_file.write("\n")
    65 |             context_file.write(f"FILE: {relative_path}\n")
    66 |             context_file.write("-" * 80)
    67 |             context_file.write("\n")
    68 | 
    69 |             if is_binary(path):
    70 |                 context_file.write("[Binary file omitted]\n\n")
    71 |                 binary_files += 1
    72 |                 continue
    73 | 
    74 |             content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    75 |             for line_number, line in enumerate(content.splitlines(), start=1):
    76 |                 context_file.write(f"{line_number:6d} | {line}\n")
    77 |             context_file.write("\n")
    78 | 
    79 |     return len(files), binary_files
    80 | 
    81 | 
    82 | def parse_arguments() -> argparse.Namespace:
    83 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
    84 |         description="Combine project files into one line-numbered context file."
    85 |     )
    86 |     parser.add_argument(
    87 |         "directory",
    88 |         nargs="?",
    89 |         type=Path,
    90 |         default=Path.cwd(),
    91 |         help="Directory to scan (default: current directory).",
    92 |     )
    93 |     parser.add_argument(
    94 |         "output",
    95 |         nargs="?",
    96 |         type=Path,
    97 |         default=Path("context.txt"),
    98 |         help="Output text file (default: context.txt).",
    99 |     )
   100 |     parser.add_argument(
   101 |         "--include-generated",
   102 |         action="store_true",
   103 |         help="Include node_modules, dist, virtual environments, and caches.",
   104 |     )
   105 |     return parser.parse_args()
   106 | 
   107 | 
   108 | def main() -> None:
   109 |     arguments = parse_arguments()
   110 |     root = arguments.directory.resolve()
   111 |     output = arguments.output.resolve()
   112 | 
   113 |     if not root.is_dir():
   114 |         raise SystemExit(f"Directory does not exist: {root}")
   115 | 
   116 |     output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   117 |     file_count, binary_count = combine_files(
   118 |         root,
   119 |         output,
   120 |         arguments.include_generated,
   121 |     )
   122 | 
   123 |     print(f"Wrote {output} with {file_count} files.")
   124 |     print(f"Omitted {binary_count} binary files.")
   125 | 
   126 | 
   127 | if __name__ == "__main__":
   128 |     main()

    27 |     <script>
    28 |       // Apply theme before first paint to avoid flash.
    29 |       (function () {
    30 |         try {
    31 |           var stored = localStorage.getItem('meridian-theme');
    32 |           var prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
    33 |           var theme = stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark' ? stored : prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
    34 |           document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
    35 |           document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = theme;
    36 |           var meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    37 |           if (meta) meta.setAttribute('content', theme === 'dark' ? '#141210' : '#f5f1e9');
    38 |         } catch (e) {}
    39 |       })();
    40 |     </script>
    41 |   </head>
    42 |   <body>

================================================================================
FILE: README.md
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | # The Meridian Archive — A Virtual Museum
     2 | 
     3 | A fictional virtual museum built with **React 19**, **TypeScript**, **React Router 7**, and **Vite 7**. Every route is a room; every object record is an invitation to look more closely.
     4 | 
     5 | ## Requirements
     6 | 
     7 | - Node.js 20.19+, 22.13+, or 24+
     8 | - npm 10+ (or pnpm / yarn / bun)
     9 | 
    10 | ## Getting started
    11 | 
    12 | ```bash
    13 | npm install
    14 | npm run dev          # http://localhost:5173

FILE: src/App.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { lazy, Suspense } from 'react';
     2 | import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { AppShell } from './components/AppShell';
     4 | import { CommandPaletteProvider } from './context/CommandPaletteContext';
     5 | import { FavoritesProvider } from './context/FavoritesContext';
     6 | import { RecentlyViewedProvider } from './context/RecentlyViewedContext';
     7 | import { ThemeProvider } from './context/ThemeContext';
     8 | import { Home } from './pages/Home';
     9 | import { NotFoundPage } from './pages/NotFoundPage';
    10 | 
    11 | // Route-level code splitting: Home is eager (it's the landing page),
    12 | // everything else is loaded on demand.
    13 | const AboutPage = lazy(() =>
    14 |   import('./pages/AboutPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.AboutPage })),
    15 | );
    16 | const ArtistDetailPage = lazy(() =>
    17 |   import('./pages/ArtistDetailPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ArtistDetailPage })),
    18 | );
    19 | const ArtistsPage = lazy(() =>
    20 |   import('./pages/ArtistsPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ArtistsPage })),
    21 | );
    22 | const ArtworkDetailPage = lazy(() =>
    23 |   import('./pages/ArtworkDetailPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ArtworkDetailPage })),
    24 | );
    25 | const CollectionsPage = lazy(() =>
    26 |   import('./pages/CollectionsPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.CollectionsPage })),
    27 | );
    28 | const ExhibitionDetailPage = lazy(() =>
    29 |   import('./pages/ExhibitionDetailPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ExhibitionDetailPage })),
    30 | );
    31 | const ExhibitionsPage = lazy(() =>
    32 |   import('./pages/ExhibitionsPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ExhibitionsPage })),
    33 | );
    34 | const FavoritesPage = lazy(() =>
    35 |   import('./pages/FavoritesPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.FavoritesPage })),
    36 | );
    37 | const SearchPage = lazy(() =>
    38 |   import('./pages/SearchPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.SearchPage })),
    39 | );
    40 | const TimelinePage = lazy(() =>
    41 |   import('./pages/TimelinePage').then((module) => ({ default: module.TimelinePage })),
    42 | );
    43 | 
    44 | function RouteFallback() {
    45 |   return (
    46 |     <div className="route-fallback" role="status" aria-live="polite">
    47 |       <span className="route-fallback-mark" aria-hidden="true">M</span>
    48 |       <p>Opening the room…</p>
    49 |     </div>
    50 |   );
    51 | }
    52 | 
    53 | export default function App() {
    54 |   return (
    55 |     <ThemeProvider>
    56 |       <FavoritesProvider>
    57 |         <RecentlyViewedProvider>
    58 |           <CommandPaletteProvider>
    59 |             <Suspense fallback={<RouteFallback />}>
    60 |               <Routes>
    61 |                 {/* Layout route: AppShell renders once and <Outlet /> swaps pages. */}
    62 |                 <Route element={<AppShell />}>
    63 |                   <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
    64 |                   <Route path="/exhibitions" element={<ExhibitionsPage />} />
    65 |                   <Route path="/exhibitions/:slug" element={<ExhibitionDetailPage />} />
    66 |                   <Route path="/works/:artworkId" element={<ArtworkDetailPage />} />
    67 |                   <Route path="/collections" element={<CollectionsPage />} />
    68 |                   <Route path="/artists" element={<ArtistsPage />} />
    69 |                   <Route path="/artists/:artistId" element={<ArtistDetailPage />} />
    70 |                   <Route path="/timeline" element={<TimelinePage />} />
    71 |                   <Route path="/search" element={<SearchPage />} />
    72 |                   <Route path="/about" element={<AboutPage />} />
    73 |                   <Route path="/favorites" element={<FavoritesPage />} />
    74 |                   <Route path="/404" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
    75 |                   <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/404" replace />} />
    76 |                 </Route>
    77 |               </Routes>
    78 |             </Suspense>
    79 |           </CommandPaletteProvider>
    80 |         </RecentlyViewedProvider>
    81 |       </FavoritesProvider>
    82 |     </ThemeProvider>
    83 |   );
    84 | }

FILE: src/components/AppShell.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect } from 'react';
     2 | import { Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { Header } from './Header';
     4 | import { Footer } from './Footer';
     5 | import { CommandPalette } from './CommandPalette';
     6 | import { usePageEffects } from '../hooks/usePageEffects';
     7 | 
     8 | const titles: Record<string, string> = {
     9 |   '/': 'The Meridian Archive — A Virtual Museum',
    10 |   '/exhibitions': 'Exhibitions — The Meridian Archive',
    11 |   '/collections': 'Collections — The Meridian Archive',
    12 |   '/artists': 'Artists & Creators — The Meridian Archive',
    13 |   '/timeline': 'Timeline — The Meridian Archive',
    14 |   '/search': 'Search — The Meridian Archive',
    15 |   '/about': 'About — The Meridian Archive',
    16 |   '/favorites': 'My Collection — The Meridian Archive',
    17 |   '/404': 'Not found — The Meridian Archive',
    18 | };
    19 | 
    20 | export function AppShell() {
    21 |   usePageEffects();
    22 |   const { pathname } = useLocation();
    23 | 
    24 |   useEffect(() => {
    25 |     document.title = titles[pathname] ?? 'The Meridian Archive';
    26 |   }, [pathname]);
    27 | 
    28 |   return (
    29 |     <>
    30 |       <a className="skip-link" href="#main-content">Skip to main content</a>
    31 |       <Header />
    32 |       <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1}>
    33 |         <Outlet />
    34 |       </main>
    35 |       <Footer />
    36 |       <CommandPalette />
    37 |     </>
    38 |   );
    39 | }

FILE: src/components/ArtworkCard.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     2 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     3 | import type { Artwork } from '../types';
     4 | import { getArtist } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | export function ArtworkCard({ artwork, variant = 'standard' }: { artwork: Artwork; variant?: 'standard' | 'feature' | 'compact' }) {
     7 |   const artist = getArtist(artwork.artistId);
     8 |   const { isFavorite, toggleFavorite } = useFavorites();
     9 |   const favorite = isFavorite(artwork.id);
    10 | 
    11 |   return (
    12 |     <article className={`artwork-card artwork-card-${variant}`}>
    13 |       <Link className="artwork-card-image" to={`/works/${artwork.id}`} aria-label={`View ${artwork.title}`}>
    14 |         <img src={artwork.image} alt={artwork.title} loading={variant === 'feature' ? 'eager' : 'lazy'} />
    15 |         {artwork.featured && <span className="image-flag">Featured</span>}
    16 |       </Link>
    17 |       <div className="artwork-card-body">
    18 |         <div className="artwork-card-topline">
    19 |           <span className="eyebrow">{artwork.category}</span>
    20 |           <button
    21 |             className={`favorite-button${favorite ? ' is-favorite' : ''}`}
    22 |             type="button"
    23 |             onClick={() => toggleFavorite(artwork.id)}
    24 |             aria-label={favorite ? `Remove ${artwork.title} from my collection` : `Add ${artwork.title} to my collection`}
    25 |             aria-pressed={favorite}
    26 |           >
    27 |             <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 21s-7.5-4.7-9.5-9C1 8.5 3 5 6.5 5c2 0 3.5 1 4.5 2.7C12 6 13.5 5 15.5 5 19 5 21 8.5 20.5 12c-1 4.3-8.5 9-8.5 9Z" /></svg>
    28 |           </button>
    29 |         </div>
    30 |         <h3><Link to={`/works/${artwork.id}`}>{artwork.title}</Link></h3>
    31 |         <p className="artwork-credit">{artist?.name} · {artwork.date}</p>
    32 |         {variant !== 'compact' && <p className="artwork-card-description">{artwork.description}</p>}
    33 |       </div>
    34 |     </article>
    35 |   );
    36 | }

     1 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

     3 | export function Breadcrumbs({ items }: { items: { label: string; to?: string }[] }) {
     4 |   return (
     5 |     <nav className="breadcrumbs" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
     6 |       <Link to="/">Home</Link>
     7 |       {items.map((item, index) => (
     8 |         <span key={`${item.label}-${index}`}>
     9 |           <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
    10 |           {item.to ? <Link to={item.to}>{item.label}</Link> : <span aria-current="page">{item.label}</span>}
    11 |         </span>
    12 |       ))}
    13 |     </nav>
    14 |   );
    15 | }

FILE: src/components/CommandPalette.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { useCommandPalette } from '../context/CommandPaletteContext';
     4 | import { artists, artworks, collections, exhibitions, timeline } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | type Command = {
     7 |   id: string;
     8 |   label: string;
     9 |   hint: string;
    10 |   group: string;
    11 |   to: string;
    12 | };
    13 | 
    14 | const MAX_RESULTS = 12;
    15 | 
    16 | function buildCommandIndex(): Command[] {
    17 |   const commands: Command[] = [];
    18 | 
    19 |   artworks.forEach((work) =>
    20 |     commands.push({
    21 |       id: `work-${work.id}`,
    22 |       label: work.title,
    23 |       hint: `${work.category} · ${work.date}`,
    24 |       group: 'Works',
    25 |       to: `/works/${work.id}`,
    26 |     }),
    27 |   );
    28 | 
    29 |   artists.forEach((artist) =>
    30 |     commands.push({
    31 |       id: `artist-${artist.id}`,
    32 |       label: artist.name,
    33 |       hint: `${artist.movement} · ${artist.nationality}`,
    34 |       group: 'Artists',
    35 |       to: `/artists/${artist.id}`,
    36 |     }),
    37 |   );
    38 | 
    39 |   exhibitions.forEach((exhibition) =>
    40 |     commands.push({
    41 |       id: `exhibition-${exhibition.id}`,
    42 |       label: exhibition.title,
    43 |       hint: `Exhibition · ${exhibition.period}`,
    44 |       group: 'Exhibitions',
    45 |       to: `/exhibitions/${exhibition.slug}`,
    46 |     }),
    47 |   );
    48 | 
    49 |   collections.forEach((collection) =>
    50 |     commands.push({
    51 |       id: `collection-${collection.id}`,
    52 |       label: collection.name,
    53 |       hint: `Collection · ${collection.workIds.length} works`,
    54 |       group: 'Collections',
    55 |       to: `/collections#${collection.slug}`,
    56 |     }),
    57 |   );
    58 | 
    59 |   timeline.forEach((event) =>
    60 |     commands.push({
    61 |       id: `event-${event.id}`,
    62 |       label: event.title,
    63 |       hint: `${event.year} · ${event.period}`,
    64 |       group: 'Timeline',
    65 |       to: event.artworkId ? `/works/${event.artworkId}` : '/timeline',
    66 |     }),
    67 |   );
    68 | 
    69 |   const navItems: { id: string; label: string; hint: string; to: string }[] = [
    70 |     { id: 'nav-home', label: 'Home', hint: 'Entrance', to: '/' },
    71 |     { id: 'nav-exhibitions', label: 'Exhibitions', hint: 'Program', to: '/exhibitions' },
    72 |     { id: 'nav-collections', label: 'Collections', hint: 'Browse', to: '/collections' },
    73 |     { id: 'nav-artists', label: 'Artists', hint: 'Directory', to: '/artists' },
    74 |     { id: 'nav-timeline', label: 'Timeline', hint: 'History', to: '/timeline' },
    75 |     { id: 'nav-search', label: 'Search', hint: 'Find anything', to: '/search' },
    76 |     { id: 'nav-favorites', label: 'My Collection', hint: 'Saved works', to: '/favorites' },
    77 |     { id: 'nav-about', label: 'About', hint: 'Our story', to: '/about' },
    78 |   ];
    79 | 
    80 |   navItems.forEach((item) => commands.push({ ...item, group: 'Navigate' }));
    81 | 
    82 |   return commands;
    83 | }
    84 | 
    85 | export function CommandPalette() {
    86 |   const { isOpen, closePalette } = useCommandPalette();
    87 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
    88 |   const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
    89 |   const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    90 |   const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
    91 |   const navigate = useNavigate();
    92 |   const index = useMemo(buildCommandIndex, []);
    93 | 
    94 |   const results = useMemo(() => {
    95 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    96 |     const pool = normalized
    97 |       ? index.filter((command) =>
    98 |           `${command.label} ${command.hint} ${command.group}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized),
    99 |         )
   100 |       : index;
   101 |     return pool.slice(0, MAX_RESULTS);
   102 |   }, [index, query]);
   103 | 
   104 |   // Focus management + scroll lock while open.
   105 |   useEffect(() => {
   106 |     if (!isOpen) {
   107 |       setQuery('');
   108 |       setActiveIndex(0);
   109 |       return;
   110 |     }
   111 |     returnFocusRef.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
   112 |     const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
   113 |     document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
   114 |     const timer = window.setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 0);
   115 |     return () => {
   116 |       window.clearTimeout(timer);
   117 |       document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
   118 |       returnFocusRef.current?.focus();
   119 |     };
   120 |   }, [isOpen]);
   121 | 
   122 |   useEffect(() => {
   123 |     setActiveIndex(0);
   124 |   }, [query]);
   125 | 
   126 |   if (!isOpen) return null;
   127 | 
   128 |   const runCommand = (command: Command) => {
   129 |     navigate(command.to);
   130 |     closePalette();
   131 |   };
   132 | 
   133 |   const handleInputKeyDown = (event: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
   134 |     if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') {
   135 |       event.preventDefault();
   136 |       setActiveIndex((current) => (results.length ? (current + 1) % results.length : 0));
   137 |     } else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
   138 |       event.preventDefault();
   139 |       setActiveIndex((current) =>
   140 |         results.length ? (current - 1 + results.length) % results.length : 0,
   141 |       );
   142 |     } else if (event.key === 'Enter') {
   143 |       event.preventDefault();
   144 |       const target = results[activeIndex];
   145 |       if (target) runCommand(target);
   146 |     } else if (event.key === 'Escape') {
   147 |       event.preventDefault();
   148 |       closePalette();
   149 |     }
   150 |   };
   151 | 
   152 |   return (
   153 |     <div className="command-overlay" onClick={closePalette} role="presentation">
   154 |       <div
   155 |         className="command-dialog"
   156 |         role="dialog"
   157 |         aria-modal="true"
   158 |         aria-label="Command palette"
   159 |         onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
   160 |       >
   161 |         <div className="command-input-row">
   162 |           <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   163 |             <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5" />
   164 |             <path d="m16 16 4 4" />
   165 |           </svg>
   166 |           <input
   167 |             ref={inputRef}
   168 |             value={query}
   169 |             onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
   170 |             onKeyDown={handleInputKeyDown}
   171 |             placeholder="Search works, artists, exhibitions, or pages…"
   172 |             aria-label="Search commands"
   173 |             autoComplete="off"
   174 |           />
   175 |           <kbd>Esc</kbd>
   176 |         </div>
   177 | 
   178 |         {results.length > 0 ? (
   179 |           <ul className="command-list" role="listbox">
   180 |             {results.map((command, i) => (
   181 |               <li key={command.id}>
   182 |                 <button
   183 |                   type="button"
   184 |                   className={i === activeIndex ? 'is-active' : ''}
   185 |                   onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIndex(i)}
   186 |                   onClick={() => runCommand(command)}
   187 |                   role="option"
   188 |                   aria-selected={i === activeIndex}
   189 |                 >
   190 |                   <span className="command-group">{command.group}</span>
   191 |                   <span className="command-label">{command.label}</span>
   192 |                   <span className="command-hint">{command.hint}</span>
   193 |                 </button>
   194 |               </li>
   195 |             ))}
   196 |           </ul>
   197 |         ) : (
   198 |           <p className="command-empty">No matches. Try “light”, “Egypt”, or “Van Gogh”.</p>
   199 |         )}
   200 | 
   201 |         <div className="command-footer">
   202 |           <span><kbd>↑</kbd><kbd>↓</kbd> navigate</span>
   203 |           <span><kbd>↵</kbd> open</span>
   204 |           <span><kbd>Esc</kbd> close</span>
   205 |         </div>
   206 |       </div>
   207 |     </div>
   208 |   );
   209 | }

FILE: src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | type Props = { children: ReactNode };
     4 | type State = { error: Error | null };
     5 | 
     6 | export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
     7 |   state: State = { error: null };
     8 | 
     9 |   static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    10 |     return { error };
    11 |   }
    12 | 
    13 |   componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo) {
    14 |     if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
    15 |       console.error('Uncaught render error:', error, info.componentStack);
    16 |     }
    17 |   }
    18 | 
    19 |   private reset = () => {
    20 |     this.setState({ error: null });
    21 |     if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    22 |       window.location.assign('/');
    23 |     }
    24 |   };
    25 | 
    26 |   render() {
    27 |     if (this.state.error) {
    28 |       return (
    29 |         <div className="not-found" role="alert">
    30 |           <p className="eyebrow">Something went sideways</p>
    31 |           <h1>The gallery lights flickered.</h1>
    32 |           <p>An unexpected error interrupted this view. You can return to the museum entrance.</p>
    33 |           <button className="button button-dark" type="button" onClick={this.reset}>
    34 |             Return to the museum
    35 |           </button>
    36 |         </div>
    37 |       );
    38 |     }
    39 |     return this.props.children;
    40 |   }
    41 | }

     1 | import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, NavLink, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     4 | import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
     5 | import { useCommandPalette } from '../context/CommandPaletteContext';
     6 | 
     7 | const primaryLinks = [
     8 |   { to: '/exhibitions', label: 'Exhibitions' },
     9 |   { to: '/collections', label: 'Collections' },
    10 |   { to: '/artists', label: 'Artists' },
    11 |   { to: '/timeline', label: 'Timeline' },
    12 |   { to: '/about', label: 'About' },
    13 | ];
    14 | 
    15 | export function Header() {
    16 |   const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    17 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
    18 |   const location = useLocation();
    19 |   const { pathname } = location;
    20 |   const navigate = useNavigate();
    21 |   const { favorites } = useFavorites();
    22 |   const { theme, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
    23 |   const { openPalette } = useCommandPalette();
    24 | 
    25 |   useEffect(() => {
    26 |     setMenuOpen(false);
    27 |   }, [pathname]);
    28 | 
    29 |   useEffect(() => {
    30 |     document.body.classList.toggle('menu-is-open', menuOpen);
    31 |     return () => document.body.classList.remove('menu-is-open');
    32 |   }, [menuOpen]);
    33 | 
    34 |   useEffect(() => {
    35 |     if (!menuOpen) return;
    36 |     const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    37 |       if (event.key === 'Escape') setMenuOpen(false);
    38 |     };
    39 |     window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    40 |     return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    41 |   }, [menuOpen]);
    42 | 
    43 |   // Keep the search field in sync with the URL when on the search page.
    44 |   useEffect(() => {
    45 |     if (pathname !== '/search') return;
    46 |     const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    47 |     setQuery(params.get('q') ?? '');
    48 |   }, [pathname, location.search]);
    49 | 
    50 |   const submitSearch = (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    51 |     event.preventDefault();
    52 |     const value = query.trim();
    53 |     navigate(value ? `/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(value)}` : '/search');
    54 |     setMenuOpen(false);
    55 |   };
    56 | 
    57 |   return (
    58 |     <header className="site-header">
    59 |       <div className="header-inner">
    60 |         <Link className="brand" to="/" aria-label="The Meridian Archive home">
    61 |           <span className="brand-mark" aria-hidden="true">M</span>
    62 |           <span>
    63 |             <strong>Meridian</strong>
    64 |             <small>Archive</small>
    65 |           </span>
    66 |         </Link>
    67 | 
    68 |         <nav
    69 |           id="primary-navigation"
    70 |           className={`primary-nav${menuOpen ? ' is-open' : ''}`}
    71 |           aria-label="Primary navigation"
    72 |         >
    73 |           <div className="mobile-nav-top">
    74 |             <span>Explore the archive</span>
    75 |             <button
    76 |               className="icon-button menu-close"
    77 |               type="button"
    78 |               onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
    79 |               aria-label="Close navigation"
    80 |             >
    81 |               ×
    82 |             </button>
    83 |           </div>
    84 | 
    85 |           <form className="mobile-search" role="search" onSubmit={submitSearch}>
    86 |             <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="mobile-site-search">Search the museum</label>
    87 |             <input
    88 |               id="mobile-site-search"
    89 |               value={query}
    90 |               onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
    91 |               placeholder="Search the archive"
    92 |               type="search"
    93 |             />
    94 |             <button type="submit" aria-label="Submit search">→</button>
    95 |           </form>
    96 | 
    97 |           {primaryLinks.map((link) => (
    98 |             <NavLink key={link.to} to={link.to}>
    99 |               {link.label}
   100 |             </NavLink>
   101 |           ))}
   102 |           <Link className="mobile-favorites-link" to="/favorites">
   103 |             My collection <span>{favorites.length}</span>

   105 |         </nav>
   106 |

   109 |             className="command-trigger"
   110 |             type="button"
   111 |             onClick={openPalette}
   112 |             aria-label="Open quick find"
   113 |           >
   114 |             <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   115 |               <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5" />
   116 |               <path d="m16 16 4 4" />
   117 |             </svg>
   118 |             <span>Quick find</span>
   119 |             <kbd>⌘K</kbd>
   120 |           </button>
   121 | 
   122 |           <button
   123 |             className="theme-toggle"
   124 |             type="button"

   126 |             aria-label={theme === 'dark' ? 'Switch to light mode' : 'Switch to dark mode'}

   128 |           >

   133 |               </svg>

   139 |           </button>
   140 |

   142 |             <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="site-search">Search the museum</label>
   143 |             <input
   144 |               id="site-search"
   145 |               value={query}

================================================================================
FILE: src/context/CommandPaletteContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | type CommandPaletteContextValue = {
     4 |   isOpen: boolean;
     5 |   openPalette: () => void;
     6 |   closePalette: () => void;
     7 |   togglePalette: () => void;
     8 | };
     9 | 
    10 | const CommandPaletteContext = createContext<CommandPaletteContextValue | null>(null);
    11 | 
    12 | export function CommandPaletteProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    13 |   const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    14 | 
    15 |   const openPalette = useCallback(() => setIsOpen(true), []);
    16 |   const closePalette = useCallback(() => setIsOpen(false), []);
    17 |   const togglePalette = useCallback(() => setIsOpen((current) => !current), []);
    18 | 
    19 |   // Global keyboard shortcut: ⌘K / Ctrl+K
    20 |   useEffect(() => {
    21 |     const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    22 |       if ((event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
    23 |         event.preventDefault();
    24 |         setIsOpen((current) => !current);
    25 |       }
    26 |     };
    27 |     window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    28 |     return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    29 |   }, []);
    30 | 
    31 |   const value = useMemo<CommandPaletteContextValue>(
    32 |     () => ({ isOpen, openPalette, closePalette, togglePalette }),
    33 |     [isOpen, openPalette, closePalette, togglePalette],
    34 |   );
    35 | 
    36 |   return (
    37 |     <CommandPaletteContext.Provider value={value}>
    38 |       {children}
    39 |     </CommandPaletteContext.Provider>
    40 |   );
    41 | }
    42 | 
    43 | export function useCommandPalette() {
    44 |   const context = useContext(CommandPaletteContext);
    45 |   if (!context) {
    46 |     throw new Error('useCommandPalette must be used within CommandPaletteProvider');
    47 |   }
    48 |   return context;
    49 | }

================================================================================
FILE: src/context/FavoritesContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | const favoritesKey = 'meridian-favorites';
     4 | const notesKey = 'meridian-favorite-notes';
     5 | 
     6 | type FavoritesContextValue = {
     7 |   favorites: string[];
     8 |   notes: Record<string, string>;
     9 |   isFavorite: (artworkId: string) => boolean;
    10 |   toggleFavorite: (artworkId: string) => void;
    11 |   setNote: (artworkId: string, note: string) => void;
    12 |   getNote: (artworkId: string) => string;
    13 | };
    14 | 
    15 | const FavoritesContext = createContext<FavoritesContextValue | null>(null);
    16 | 
    17 | function readFavorites(): string[] {
    18 |   try {
    19 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(favoritesKey);
    20 |     if (!stored) return [];
    21 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored);
    22 |     return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string') : [];
    23 |   } catch {
    24 |     return [];
    25 |   }
    26 | }
    27 | 
    28 | function readNotes(): Record<string, string> {
    29 |   try {
    30 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(notesKey);
    31 |     if (!stored) return {};
    32 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored);
    33 |     if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') return {};
    34 |     const result: Record<string, string> = {};
    35 |     for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(parsed as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    36 |       if (typeof value === 'string') result[key] = value;
    37 |     }
    38 |     return result;
    39 |   } catch {
    40 |     return {};
    41 |   }
    42 | }
    43 | 
    44 | export function FavoritesProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    45 |   const [favorites, setFavorites] = useState<string[]>(readFavorites);
    46 |   const [notes, setNotes] = useState<Record<string, string>>(readNotes);
    47 | 
    48 |   useEffect(() => {
    49 |     try {
    50 |       window.localStorage.setItem(favoritesKey, JSON.stringify(favorites));
    51 |     } catch {
    52 |       // Ignore storage access errors
    53 |     }
    54 |   }, [favorites]);
    55 | 
    56 |   useEffect(() => {
    57 |     try {
    58 |       window.localStorage.setItem(notesKey, JSON.stringify(notes));
    59 |     } catch {
    60 |       // Ignore storage access errors
    61 |     }
    62 |   }, [notes]);
    63 | 
    64 |   // Stable identity: safe to depend on inside useEffect.
    65 |   const toggleFavorite = useCallback((artworkId: string) => {
    66 |     setFavorites((current) =>
    67 |       current.includes(artworkId)
    68 |         ? current.filter((id) => id !== artworkId)
    69 |         : [...current, artworkId],
    70 |     );
    71 |   }, []);
    72 | 
    73 |   const setNote = useCallback((artworkId: string, note: string) => {
    74 |     setNotes((current) => {
    75 |       const trimmed = note.trim();
    76 |       if (!trimmed) {
    77 |         const { [artworkId]: _removed, ...rest } = current;
    78 |         return rest;
    79 |       }
    80 |       return { ...current, [artworkId]: trimmed };
    81 |     });
    82 |   }, []);
    83 | 
    84 |   const value = useMemo<FavoritesContextValue>(
    85 |     () => ({
    86 |       favorites,
    87 |       notes,
    88 |       isFavorite: (artworkId) => favorites.includes(artworkId),
    89 |       toggleFavorite,
    90 |       setNote,
    91 |       getNote: (artworkId) => notes[artworkId] ?? '',
    92 |     }),
    93 |     [favorites, notes, toggleFavorite, setNote],
    94 |   );
    95 | 
    96 |   return (
    97 |     <FavoritesContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</FavoritesContext.Provider>
    98 |   );
    99 | }
   100 | 
   101 | export function useFavorites() {
   102 |   const context = useContext(FavoritesContext);
   103 |   if (!context) {
   104 |     throw new Error('useFavorites must be used within FavoritesProvider');
   105 |   }
   106 |   return context;
   107 | }

================================================================================
FILE: src/context/RecentlyViewedContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | const storageKey = 'meridian-recently-viewed';
     4 | const maxItems = 8;
     5 | 
     6 | type RecentlyViewedContextValue = {
     7 |   recentIds: string[];
     8 |   recordView: (artworkId: string) => void;
     9 |   clearHistory: () => void;
    10 | };
    11 | 
    12 | const RecentlyViewedContext = createContext<RecentlyViewedContextValue | null>(null);
    13 | 
    14 | function readStoredIds(): string[] {
    15 |   if (typeof window === 'undefined') return [];
    16 |   try {
    17 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    18 |     if (!stored) return [];
    19 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored);
    20 |     if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
    21 |     return parsed.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string').slice(0, maxItems);
    22 |   } catch {
    23 |     return [];
    24 |   }
    25 | }
    26 | 
    27 | export function RecentlyViewedProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    28 |   const [recentIds, setRecentIds] = useState<string[]>(readStoredIds);
    29 | 
    30 |   useEffect(() => {
    31 |     try {
    32 |       window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(recentIds));
    33 |     } catch {
    34 |       // Ignore storage access errors
    35 |     }
    36 |   }, [recentIds]);
    37 | 
    38 |   // Stable identity so consumers can safely depend on it inside useEffect.
    39 |   const recordView = useCallback((artworkId: string) => {
    40 |     setRecentIds((current) =>
    41 |       [artworkId, ...current.filter((id) => id !== artworkId)].slice(0, maxItems),
    42 |     );
    43 |   }, []);
    44 | 
    45 |   const clearHistory = useCallback(() => setRecentIds([]), []);
    46 | 
    47 |   const value = useMemo<RecentlyViewedContextValue>(
    48 |     () => ({ recentIds, recordView, clearHistory }),
    49 |     [recentIds, recordView, clearHistory],
    50 |   );
    51 | 
    52 |   return (
    53 |     <RecentlyViewedContext.Provider value={value}>
    54 |       {children}
    55 |     </RecentlyViewedContext.Provider>
    56 |   );
    57 | }
    58 | 
    59 | export function useRecentlyViewed() {
    60 |   const context = useContext(RecentlyViewedContext);
    61 |   if (!context) {
    62 |     throw new Error('useRecentlyViewed must be used within RecentlyViewedProvider');
    63 |   }
    64 |   return context;
    65 | }

================================================================================
FILE: src/context/ThemeContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | export type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';
     4 | 
     5 | type ThemeContextValue = {
     6 |   theme: Theme;
     7 |   setTheme: (theme: Theme) => void;
     8 |   toggleTheme: () => void;
     9 | };
    10 | 
    11 | const storageKey = 'meridian-theme';
    12 | const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextValue | null>(null);
    13 | 
    14 | function getInitialTheme(): Theme {
    15 |   if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 'light';
    16 |   try {
    17 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    18 |     if (stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark') return stored;
    19 |   } catch {
    20 |     // Ignore storage access errors (private mode, etc.)
    21 |   }
    22 |   return window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light';
    23 | }
    24 | 
    25 | export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    26 |   const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(getInitialTheme);
    27 | 
    28 |   useEffect(() => {
    29 |     document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
    30 |     document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = theme;
    31 |     try {
    32 |       window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, theme);
    33 |     } catch {
    34 |       // Ignore storage access errors
    35 |     }
    36 |     const meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    37 |     if (meta) {
    38 |       meta.setAttribute('content', theme === 'dark' ? '#141210' : '#f5f1e9');
    39 |     }
    40 |   }, [theme]);
    41 | 
    42 |   const toggleTheme = useCallback(() => {
    43 |     setTheme((current) => (current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'));
    44 |   }, []);
    45 | 
    46 |   const value = useMemo<ThemeContextValue>(
    47 |     () => ({ theme, setTheme, toggleTheme }),
    48 |     [theme, toggleTheme],
    49 |   );
    50 | 
    51 |   return <ThemeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ThemeContext.Provider>;
    52 | }
    53 | 
    54 | export function useTheme() {
    55 |   const context = useContext(ThemeContext);
    56 |   if (!context) {
    57 |     throw new Error('useTheme must be used within ThemeProvider');
    58 |   }
    59 |   return context;
    60 | }

     6 |   return `https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/${encodeURIComponent(title)}?width=${width}`;
     7 | };

   674 | // --- O(1) lookups -----------------------------------------------------------
   675 | const artistsById = new Map(artists.map((artist) => [artist.id, artist]));
   676 | const artworksById = new Map(artworks.map((artwork) => [artwork.id, artwork]));
   677 | const collectionsById = new Map(collections.map((collection) => [collection.id, collection]));
   678 | const exhibitionsById = new Map(exhibitions.map((exhibition) => [exhibition.id, exhibition]));
   679 | 
   680 | export const getArtist = (id: string) => artistsById.get(id);
   681 | export const getArtwork = (id: string) => artworksById.get(id);
   682 | export const getCollection = (id: string) => collectionsById.get(id);
   683 | export const getExhibition = (id: string) => exhibitionsById.get(id);

================================================================================
FILE: src/hooks/usePageEffects.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect } from 'react';
     2 | import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | 
     4 | export function usePageEffects() {
     5 |   const { pathname, hash } = useLocation();
     6 | 
     7 |   useEffect(() => {
     8 |     // Let the browser handle in-page anchor navigation.
     9 |     if (hash) return;
    10 |     window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
    11 |   }, [pathname, hash]);
    12 | 
    13 |   useEffect(() => {
    14 |     const elements = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-reveal]');
    15 |     if (elements.length === 0) return;
    16 | 
    17 |     const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
    18 |       (entries) => {
    19 |         entries.forEach((entry) => {
    20 |           if (entry.isIntersecting) {
    21 |             entry.target.classList.add('is-visible');
    22 |             observer.unobserve(entry.target);
    23 |           }
    24 |         });
    25 |       },
    26 |       { threshold: 0.12, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px' },
    27 |     );
    28 | 
    29 |     elements.forEach((element) => observer.observe(element));
    30 |     return () => observer.disconnect();
    31 |   }, [pathname, hash]);
    32 | }

    10 |     <ErrorBoundary>

    14 |     </ErrorBoundary>
    15 |   </StrictMode>,
    16 | );

    18 |           <h2>To make looking feel like a place you can enter.</h2>
    19 |           <p>
    20 |             The Meridian Archive is a fictional virtual museum designed as a working model for digital
    21 |             interpretation. We bring together public-domain-inspired imagery, structured collection data,
    22 |             and editorial storytelling so that a visit can feel as considered as a walk through a physical
    23 |             gallery.
    24 |           </p>
    25 |         </div>
    26 |       </section>
    27 |

    34 |           <div>
    35 |             <p>
    36 |               The archive began as a simple question: what if a museum could keep the quiet, the pacing,
    37 |               and the sense of discovery of a gallery while becoming available to anyone with an internet
    38 |               connection? The result is a responsive, accessible website where every route is a room and
    39 |               every object record is an invitation to look more closely.
    40 |             </p>
    41 |             <p>
    42 |               Our content is deliberately fictional and clearly identified as such. Image sources are

    55 |             <p>
    56 |               We favor slow looking over endless scrolling. Each exhibition is built around a question,

    58 |               uncertainty.
    59 |             </p>
    60 |           </div>
    61 |           <div>

FILE: src/pages/ArtistsPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { ArtistCard } from '../components/ArtistCard';
     3 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { artists } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | export function ArtistsPage() {
     7 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
     8 |   const [movement, setMovement] = useState('All');
     9 |   const movements = ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(artists.map((artist) => artist.movement)))];
    10 | 
    11 |   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    12 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    13 |     return artists.filter((artist) => {
    14 |       const matchesQuery = !normalized || `${artist.name} ${artist.bio} ${artist.nationality} ${artist.movement}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized);
    15 |       return matchesQuery && (movement === 'All' || artist.movement === movement);
    16 |     });
    17 |   }, [query, movement]);
    18 | 
    19 |   return (
    20 |     <>
    21 |       <PageHero eyebrow="Creator directory" title="The hands behind the hours" description="Meet the artists, workshops, and makers whose decisions continue to shape the way we see." image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/Vincent%20van%20Gogh%20-%20Self-Portrait%20-%20Google%20Art%20Project.jpg?width=1280" imageAlt="Portrait of Vincent van Gogh" />
    22 |       <section className="section">
    23 |         <div className="section-inner">
    24 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    25 |             <SectionHeading eyebrow="Directory" title="Search the makers" description="Browse by name, movement, nationality, or a detail from a biography." />
    26 |             <span className="result-count">{filtered.length} {filtered.length === 1 ? 'creator' : 'creators'}</span>
    27 |           </div>
    28 |           <div className="filter-bar">
    29 |             <label className="filter-search">
    30 |               <span className="sr-only">Search artists</span>
    31 |               <input value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search artists or movements" type="search" />
    32 |             </label>
    33 |             <label>
    34 |               <span>Movement</span>
    35 |               <select value={movement} onChange={(event) => setMovement(event.target.value)}>{movements.map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}</select>
    36 |             </label>
    37 |             {(query || movement !== 'All') && <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={() => { setQuery(''); setMovement('All'); }}>Clear filters</button>}
    38 |           </div>
    39 |           {filtered.length > 0 ? (
    40 |             <div className="artist-grid">{filtered.map((artist) => <ArtistCard key={artist.id} artist={artist} />)}</div>
    41 |           ) : <EmptyState title="No creators found" description="Try a different name or broaden the movement filter to continue through the directory." action={<button className="button button-dark" type="button" onClick={() => { setQuery(''); setMovement('All'); }}>Reset the directory</button>} />}
    42 |         </div>
    43 |       </section>
    44 |     </>
    45 |   );
    46 | }

FILE: src/pages/ArtworkDetailPage.tsx

     1 | import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { Breadcrumbs } from '../components/Breadcrumbs';
     5 | import { EmptyState } from '../components/UI';
     6 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     7 | import { useRecentlyViewed } from '../context/RecentlyViewedContext';
     8 | import { artworks, getArtist, getArtwork } from '../data/museumData';
     9 | 
    10 | export function ArtworkDetailPage() {
    11 |   const { artworkId = '' } = useParams();
    12 |   const artwork = getArtwork(artworkId);
    13 |   const artist = artwork ? getArtist(artwork.artistId) : undefined;
    14 |   const { isFavorite, toggleFavorite } = useFavorites();
    15 |   const { recordView } = useRecentlyViewed();
    16 | 
    17 |   const [zoomOpen, setZoomOpen] = useState(false);
    18 |   const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
    19 |   const [shareStatus, setShareStatus] = useState('');
    20 |   const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    21 |   const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
    22 | 
    23 |   // Record this artwork in "recently viewed". recordView is stable, so this
    24 |   // effect fires only when the artwork itself changes.
    25 |   useEffect(() => {
    26 |     if (artwork) recordView(artwork.id);
    27 |   }, [artwork, recordView]);
    28 | 
    29 |   useEffect(() => {
    30 |     if (!zoomOpen) setZoom(1);
    31 |   }, [zoomOpen]);
    32 | 
    33 |   useEffect(() => {
    34 |     if (!zoomOpen) return;
    35 | 
    36 |     returnFocusRef.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    37 |     const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    38 |     document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    39 | 
    40 |     const focusTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
    41 |       dialogRef.current?.focus();
    42 |     }, 0);
    43 | 
    44 |     const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    45 |       if (event.key === 'Escape') {
    46 |         setZoomOpen(false);
    47 |         return;
    48 |       }
    49 |       if (event.key !== 'Tab' || !dialogRef.current) return;
    50 | 
    51 |       const focusable = dialogRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
    52 |         'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
    53 |       );
    54 |       if (focusable.length === 0) return;
    55 | 
    56 |       const first = focusable[0];
    57 |       const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
    58 |       const active = document.activeElement;
    59 | 
    60 |       if (event.shiftKey && active === first) {
    61 |         event.preventDefault();
    62 |         last.focus();
    63 |       } else if (!event.shiftKey && active === last) {
    64 |         event.preventDefault();
    65 |         first.focus();
    66 |       }
    67 |     };
    68 | 
    69 |     window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    70 |     return () => {
    71 |       window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    72 |       window.clearTimeout(focusTimer);
    73 |       document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
    74 |       returnFocusRef.current?.focus();
    75 |     };
    76 |   }, [zoomOpen]);
    77 | 
    78 |   if (!artwork || !artist) {
    79 |     return (
    80 |       <EmptyState
    81 |         title="Object not found"
    82 |         description="This record may be between installations. Return to the archive to keep exploring."
    83 |         action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/collections">Browse collections</Link>}
    84 |       />
    85 |     );
    86 |   }
    87 | 
    88 |   const related = artworks
    89 |     .filter(
    90 |       (item) =>
    91 |         item.id !== artwork.id &&
    92 |         (item.category === artwork.category ||
    93 |           item.collectionIds.some((id) => artwork.collectionIds.includes(id))),
    94 |     )
    95 |     .slice(0, 3);
    96 |   const favorite = isFavorite(artwork.id);
    97 | 
    98 |   const handleShare = async () => {
    99 |     const shareUrl = `${window.location.origin}/works/${artwork.id}`;
   100 |     const shareData = {
   101 |       title: `${artwork.title} — The Meridian Archive`,
   102 |       text: artwork.description,
   103 |       url: shareUrl,
   104 |     };
   105 | 
   106 |     try {
   107 |       if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && 'share' in navigator && typeof navigator.share === 'function') {
   108 |         await navigator.share(shareData);
   109 |         return;
   110 |       }
   111 |       if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.clipboard) {
   112 |         await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl);
   113 |         setShareStatus('Link copied to clipboard');
   114 |       } else {
   115 |         setShareStatus(`Copy this link: ${shareUrl}`);
   116 |       }
   117 |     } catch {
   118 |       setShareStatus('Unable to share right now');
   119 |     }
   120 |     window.setTimeout(() => setShareStatus(''), 2600);
   121 |   };
   122 | 
   123 |   return (
   124 |     <>
   125 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Collections', to: '/collections' }, { label: artwork.title }]} />
   126 |       <section className="artwork-detail">
   127 |         <div className="artwork-viewer">
   128 |           <button
   129 |             className="artwork-viewer-image"
   130 |             type="button"
   131 |             onClick={() => setZoomOpen(true)}
   132 |             aria-label={`Open ${artwork.title} in image viewer`}
   133 |           >
   134 |             <img src={artwork.image} alt={artwork.title} />
   135 |             <span className="viewer-hint">Click to enlarge</span>
   136 |           </button>
   137 |           <div className="zoom-controls" aria-label="Image viewer controls">
   138 |             <button type="button" onClick={() => setZoomOpen(true)} aria-label="Enlarge image">+</button>
   139 |             <span>View details</span>
   140 |           </div>
   141 |         </div>
   142 |         <div className="artwork-detail-copy">
   143 |           <p className="eyebrow">{artwork.category} · {artwork.period}</p>
   144 |           <h1>{artwork.title}</h1>
   145 |           <p className="artwork-artist-line"><Link to={`/artists/${artist.id}`}>{artist.name}</Link></p>
   146 |           <p className="artwork-detail-description">{artwork.description}</p>
   147 |           <dl className="artwork-facts">
   148 |             <div><dt>Date</dt><dd>{artwork.date}</dd></div>
   149 |             <div><dt>Medium</dt><dd>{artwork.medium}</dd></div>
   150 |             <div><dt>Dimensions</dt><dd>{artwork.dimensions}</dd></div>
   151 |             <div><dt>Location</dt><dd>{artwork.location}</dd></div>
   152 |           </dl>
   153 |           <div className="artwork-detail-actions">
   154 |             <button
   155 |               className={`button ${favorite ? 'button-dark' : 'button-outline-dark'} favorite-detail-button${favorite ? ' is-favorite' : ''}`}
   156 |               type="button"
   157 |               onClick={() => toggleFavorite(artwork.id)}
   158 |               aria-pressed={favorite}
   159 |             >
   160 |               <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   161 |                 <path d="M12 21s-7.5-4.7-9.5-9C1 8.5 3 5 6.5 5c2 0 3.5 1 4.5 2.7C12 6 13.5 5 15.5 5 19 5 21 8.5 20.5 12c-1 4.3-8.5 9-8.5 9Z" />
   162 |               </svg>
   163 |               {favorite ? 'Saved to my collection' : 'Save to my collection'}
   164 |             </button>
   165 |             <button
   166 |               className="button button-outline-dark share-detail-button"
   167 |               type="button"
   168 |               onClick={handleShare}
   169 |             >
   170 |               <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   171 |                 <circle cx="6" cy="12" r="2.5" />
   172 |                 <circle cx="18" cy="6" r="2.5" />
   173 |                 <circle cx="18" cy="18" r="2.5" />
   174 |                 <path d="m8.2 10.8 7.6-3.6M8.2 13.2l7.6 3.6" />
   175 |               </svg>
   176 |               Share
   177 |             </button>
   178 |           </div>
   179 |           {shareStatus && (
   180 |             <p className="share-status" role="status">{shareStatus}</p>
   181 |           )}
   182 |         </div>
   183 |       </section>
   184 | 
   185 |       <section className="section artwork-context">
   186 |         <div className="section-inner artwork-context-grid">
   187 |           <div>
   188 |             <p className="eyebrow">Historical context</p>
   189 |             <h2>{artwork.context}</h2>
   190 |           </div>
   191 |           <div>
   192 |             <p>{artwork.description} {artwork.context}</p>
   193 |             <p className="context-pullquote">
   194 |               “An object is never only itself. It is a record of attention, material, and the hands that
   195 |               carried it forward.”
   196 |             </p>
   197 |           </div>
   198 |         </div>
   199 |       </section>
   200 | 
   201 |       <section className="section section-tinted">
   202 |         <div className="section-inner provenance-panel">
   203 |           <div>
   204 |             <p className="eyebrow">Collection record</p>
   205 |             <h2>Provenance &amp; care</h2>
   206 |           </div>
   207 |           <div>
   208 |             <p>{artwork.provenance}</p>
   209 |             <p>
   210 |               This fictional record is intentionally concise: it models the kind of transparent,
   211 |               replaceable metadata a real collection management system might expose.
   212 |             </p>
   213 |           </div>
   214 |         </div>
   215 |       </section>
   216 | 
   217 |       <section className="section">
   218 |         <div className="section-inner">
   219 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
   220 |             <div className="related-creator">
   221 |               <p className="eyebrow">Related creator</p>

FILE: src/pages/CollectionsPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     5 | import { artworks, collections } from '../data/museumData';
     6 | 
     7 | export function CollectionsPage() {
     8 |   const { hash } = useLocation();
     9 |   const initialHash = hash.replace('#', '');
    10 |   const [activeCollection, setActiveCollection] = useState<string>(
    11 |     initialHash && collections.some((collection) => collection.id === initialHash) ? initialHash : 'all',
    12 |   );
    13 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
    14 |   const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
    15 | 
    16 |   // Keep collection state in sync with the URL hash so deep links work.
    17 |   useEffect(() => {
    18 |     const id = hash.replace('#', '');
    19 |     if (id && collections.some((collection) => collection.id === id)) {
    20 |       setActiveCollection(id);
    21 |     }
    22 |   }, [hash]);
    23 | 
    24 |   const visibleWorks = useMemo(() => {
    25 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    26 |     return artworks.filter((work) => {
    27 |       const matchesCollection =
    28 |         activeCollection === 'all' || work.collectionIds.includes(activeCollection);
    29 |       const matchesCategory = category === 'All' || work.category === category;
    30 |       const matchesQuery =
    31 |         !normalized ||
    32 |         `${work.title} ${work.medium} ${work.location} ${work.tags.join(' ')}`
    33 |           .toLowerCase()
    34 |           .includes(normalized);
    35 |       return matchesCollection && matchesCategory && matchesQuery;
    36 |     });
    37 |   }, [activeCollection, category, query]);
    38 | 
    39 |   const categories = ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(artworks.map((work) => work.category)))];
    40 | 
    41 |   return (
    42 |     <>
    43 |       <PageHero
    44 |         eyebrow="The archive"
    45 |         title="Collections for every kind of looking"
    46 |         description="Browse by material, era, or medium. Every object record is designed to be a doorway into a larger story."

   133 |                 <button
   134 |                   className="button button-dark"
   135 |                   type="button"
   136 |                   onClick={() => {
   137 |                     setQuery('');
   138 |                     setCategory('All');
   139 |                     setActiveCollection('all');

   141 |                 >
   142 |                   Reset the archive
   143 |                 </button>

   149 |

     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';

    10 | export function ExhibitionDetailPage() {
    11 |   const { slug = '' } = useParams();
    12 |   const exhibition = exhibitions.find((item) => item.slug === slug);
    13 |   const [activeWork, setActiveWork] = useState(artworks.find((work) => work.id === exhibition?.workIds[0])?.id ?? '');
    14 |   const activeArtwork = artworks.find((work) => work.id === activeWork);
    15 | 
    16 |   const relatedExhibitions = useMemo(() => exhibition?.relatedExhibitionIds.map((id) => exhibitions.find((item) => item.id === id)).filter(Boolean) ?? [], [exhibition]);
    17 |   const relatedArtists = useMemo(() => exhibition?.relatedArtistIds.map((id) => artists.find((item) => item.id === id)).filter(Boolean) ?? [], [exhibition]);
    18 |   const featuredWorks = useMemo<Artwork[]>(() => exhibition?.workIds.map((id) => artworks.find((work) => work.id === id)).filter((work): work is Artwork => Boolean(work)) ?? [], [exhibition]);
    19 | 
    20 |   if (!exhibition) {
    21 |     return <EmptyState title="Exhibition not found" description="This gallery may be between installations. Return to the exhibition program to keep exploring." action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/exhibitions">View exhibitions</Link>} />;
    22 |   }
    23 | 
    24 |   return (
    25 |     <>
    26 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Exhibitions', to: '/exhibitions' }, { label: exhibition.title }]} />

    32 |           <p>{exhibition.description}</p>

    36 |             <span><small>Visit</small>Always open</span>
    37 |           </div>
    38 |         </div>
    39 |         <div className="exhibition-intro-image" data-reveal>
    40 |           <img src={exhibition.coverImage} alt="" />

FILE: src/pages/ExhibitionsPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { ExhibitionCard } from '../components/ExhibitionCard';
     3 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { exhibitions } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | export function ExhibitionsPage() {
     7 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
     8 |   const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
     9 |   const [period, setPeriod] = useState('All');
    10 | 
    11 |   const categories = useMemo(
    12 |     () => ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(exhibitions.map((exhibition) => exhibition.category)))],
    13 |     [],
    14 |   );
    15 |   const periods = useMemo(
    16 |     () => ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(exhibitions.map((exhibition) => exhibition.period)))],
    17 |     [],
    18 |   );
    19 | 
    20 |   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    21 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    22 |     return exhibitions.filter((exhibition) => {
    23 |       const matchesQuery =
    24 |         !normalized ||
    25 |         `${exhibition.title} ${exhibition.dek} ${exhibition.description} ${exhibition.curator}`
    26 |           .toLowerCase()
    27 |           .includes(normalized);
    28 |       const matchesCategory = category === 'All' || exhibition.category === category;
    29 |       const matchesPeriod = period === 'All' || exhibition.period === period;
    30 |       return matchesQuery && matchesCategory && matchesPeriod;
    31 |     });
    32 |   }, [query, category, period]);
    33 | 
    34 |   const featuredExhibition = filtered.find((exhibition) => exhibition.featured);
    35 |   const remainingExhibitions = featuredExhibition
    36 |     ? filtered.filter((exhibition) => exhibition.id !== featuredExhibition.id)
    37 |     : filtered;
    38 | 
    39 |   const hasActiveFilters = query !== '' || category !== 'All' || period !== 'All';
    40 | 
    41 |   const resetFilters = () => {
    42 |     setQuery('');
    43 |     setCategory('All');
    44 |     setPeriod('All');
    45 |   };
    46 | 
    47 |   return (
    48 |     <>
    49 |       <PageHero
    50 |         eyebrow="Exhibition program"
    51 |         title="Galleries built around a question"
    52 |         description="Each exhibition is a sequence of encounters—begin anywhere, follow what catches your eye, and let one object lead you to the next."
    53 |         image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/Van%20Gogh%20-%20Starry%20Night%20-%20Google%20Art%20Project.jpg?width=1280"
    54 |         imageAlt="A painted night sky above a quiet village"
    55 |       />
    56 | 
    57 |       <section className="section">
    58 |         <div className="section-inner">
    59 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    60 |             <SectionHeading
    61 |               eyebrow="Now showing"
    62 |               title="Exhibitions with room to wander"
    63 |               description="Filter the program by subject or period, or search for a curator, a theme, or an idea."
    64 |             />
    65 |             <span className="result-count">
    66 |               {filtered.length} {filtered.length === 1 ? 'exhibition' : 'exhibitions'}
    67 |             </span>
    68 |           </div>
    69 | 
    70 |           <div className="filter-bar">
    71 |             <label className="filter-search">
    72 |               <span className="sr-only">Search exhibitions</span>
    73 |               <input
    74 |                 value={query}
    75 |                 onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
    76 |                 placeholder="Search exhibitions, curators, or themes"
    77 |                 type="search"
    78 |               />
    79 |             </label>
    80 |             <label>
    81 |               <span>Subject</span>
    82 |               <select value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}>
    83 |                 {categories.map((item) => (
    84 |                   <option key={item}>{item}</option>
    85 |                 ))}
    86 |               </select>
    87 |             </label>
    88 |             <label>
    89 |               <span>Period</span>
    90 |               <select value={period} onChange={(event) => setPeriod(event.target.value)}>
    91 |                 {periods.map((item) => (
    92 |                   <option key={item}>{item}</option>
    93 |                 ))}
    94 |               </select>
    95 |             </label>
    96 |             {hasActiveFilters && (
    97 |               <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={resetFilters}>
    98 |                 Clear filters
    99 |               </button>
   100 |             )}
   101 |           </div>
   102 | 
   103 |           {filtered.length > 0 ? (
   104 |             <>
   105 |               {featuredExhibition && (
   106 |                 <div className="exhibition-grid exhibition-grid-featured">
   107 |                   <ExhibitionCard exhibition={featuredExhibition} featured />
   108 |                 </div>
   109 |               )}
   110 |               {remainingExhibitions.length > 0 && (
   111 |                 <div className="exhibition-grid exhibition-grid-results">
   112 |                   {remainingExhibitions.map((exhibition) => (
   113 |                     <ExhibitionCard key={exhibition.id} exhibition={exhibition} />
   114 |                   ))}
   115 |                 </div>
   116 |               )}
   117 |             </>
   118 |           ) : (
   119 |             <EmptyState
   120 |               title="No exhibitions found"
   121 |               description="Try a different search or broaden the subject and period filters to continue through the program."
   122 |               action={
   123 |                 <button className="button button-dark" type="button" onClick={resetFilters}>
   124 |                   Reset the program
   125 |                 </button>
   126 |               }
   127 |             />
   128 |           )}
   129 |         </div>
   130 |       </section>
   131 | 
   132 |       <section className="section section-tinted">
   133 |         <div className="section-inner collection-note-grid">
   134 |           <div>
   135 |             <p className="eyebrow">A living program</p>
   136 |             <h2>Every gallery is a working draft.</h2>
   137 |           </div>
   138 |           <p>
   139 |             Exhibitions in the Meridian Archive are designed to be replaced, reinterpreted, and
   140 |             re-curated. The data model, object records, and editorial voice are all kept separate so a
   141 |             real institution can swap in new stories without rebuilding the visitor experience.
   142 |           </p>
   143 |         </div>
   144 |       </section>
   145 |     </>
   146 |   );
   147 | }

FILE: src/pages/FavoritesPage.tsx

     1 | import { useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     5 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     6 | import { useRecentlyViewed } from '../context/RecentlyViewedContext';
     7 | import { artworks, getArtwork } from '../data/museumData';
     8 | import type { Artwork } from '../types';
     9 | 
    10 | export function FavoritesPage() {
    11 |   const { favorites, getNote, setNote } = useFavorites();
    12 |   const { recentIds, clearHistory } = useRecentlyViewed();
    13 | 
    14 |   const favoriteWorks = artworks.filter((work) => favorites.includes(work.id));
    15 |   const recentWorks: Artwork[] = recentIds
    16 |     .map((id) => getArtwork(id))
    17 |     .filter((work): work is Artwork => Boolean(work))
    18 |     .slice(0, 4);
    19 | 
    20 |   return (
    21 |     <>
    22 |       <PageHero
    23 |         eyebrow="Personal collection"
    24 |         title="The works you are carrying"
    25 |         description="Your saved objects live in this browser and remain here between visits."
    26 |         image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/Dragonfly%20Lamp%2C%20Tiffany%20Studios%2C%20Dayton%20Art%20Institute.jpg?width=1280"
    27 |         imageAlt="A leaded-glass lamp glowing in a dark room"
    28 |       />
    29 | 
    30 |       <section className="section favorites-page">
    31 |         <div className="section-inner">
    32 |           <SectionHeading
    33 |             eyebrow="My collection"
    34 |             title={
    35 |               favoriteWorks.length
    36 |                 ? `${favoriteWorks.length} saved ${favoriteWorks.length === 1 ? 'object' : 'objects'}`
    37 |                 : 'A collection begins with one look'
    38 |             }
    39 |             description={
    40 |               favoriteWorks.length
    41 |                 ? 'Add a private note to any saved work to remember why it matters, or remove it to keep your collection focused.'
    42 |                 : 'Save an artwork from its detail page and it will appear here as a personal path through the museum.'
    43 |             }
    44 |           />
    45 |           {favoriteWorks.length > 0 ? (
    46 |             <div className="favorites-list">
    47 |               {favoriteWorks.map((work, index) => (
    48 |                 <div className="favorite-entry" key={work.id}>
    49 |                   <ArtworkCard
    50 |                     artwork={work}
    51 |                     variant={index === 0 ? 'feature' : 'standard'}
    52 |                   />
    53 |                   <FavoriteNoteEditor
    54 |                     artworkId={work.id}
    55 |                     note={getNote(work.id)}
    56 |                     onSave={(value) => setNote(work.id, value)}
    57 |                   />
    58 |                 </div>
    59 |               ))}
    60 |             </div>
    61 |           ) : (
    62 |             <EmptyState
    63 |               title="Your collection is empty"
    64 |               description="There are no saved objects yet. Find an artwork you want to return to and choose Save to my collection."
    65 |               action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/collections">Start exploring</Link>}
    66 |             />
    67 |           )}
    68 |         </div>
    69 |       </section>
    70 | 
    71 |       {recentWorks.length > 0 && (
    72 |         <section className="section section-tinted recently-viewed-section">
    73 |           <div className="section-inner">
    74 |             <div className="section-heading-row">
    75 |               <SectionHeading
    76 |                 eyebrow="Your path"
    77 |                 title="Recently viewed"
    78 |                 description="A quiet record of where you have been. Clear it whenever you like."
    79 |               />
    80 |               <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={clearHistory}>
    81 |                 Clear history
    82 |               </button>
    83 |             </div>
    84 |             <div className="artwork-grid artwork-grid-three">
    85 |               {recentWorks.map((work) => (
    86 |                 <ArtworkCard key={work.id} artwork={work} variant="compact" />
    87 |               ))}
    88 |             </div>
    89 |           </div>
    90 |         </section>
    91 |       )}
    92 |     </>
    93 |   );
    94 | }
    95 | 
    96 | function FavoriteNoteEditor({
    97 |   artworkId,
    98 |   note,
    99 |   onSave,
   100 | }: {
   101 |   artworkId: string;
   102 |   note: string;
   103 |   onSave: (value: string) => void;
   104 | }) {
   105 |   const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
   106 |   const [draft, setDraft] = useState(note);
   107 | 
   108 |   const startEditing = () => {
   109 |     setDraft(note);
   110 |     setEditing(true);
   111 |   };
   112 | 
   113 |   const save = () => {
   114 |     onSave(draft);
   115 |     setEditing(false);
   116 |   };
   117 | 
   118 |   const cancel = () => {
   119 |     setDraft(note);
   120 |     setEditing(false);
   121 |   };
   122 | 
   123 |   if (editing) {
   124 |     return (
   125 |       <div className="favorite-note-editor">
   126 |         <label className="sr-only" htmlFor={`note-${artworkId}`}>
   127 |           Personal note for this artwork
   128 |         </label>
   129 |         <textarea
   130 |           id={`note-${artworkId}`}
   131 |           value={draft}
   132 |           onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)}
   133 |           rows={3}
   134 |           placeholder="A memory, a question, a reason to return…"
   135 |         />
   136 |         <div className="favorite-note-actions">
   137 |           <button type="button" className="button button-dark" onClick={save}>
   138 |             Save note
   139 |           </button>
   140 |           <button type="button" className="filter-clear" onClick={cancel}>
   141 |             Cancel
   142 |           </button>
   143 |         </div>
   144 |       </div>
   145 |     );
   146 |   }
   147 | 
   148 |   return (
   149 |     <button type="button" className="favorite-note-toggle" onClick={startEditing}>
   150 |       <span className="eyebrow">{note ? 'Your note' : 'Add a note'}</span>
   151 |       <span className="favorite-note-preview">
   152 |         {note || 'Capture a thought about this work…'}
   153 |       </span>
   154 |     </button>
   155 |   );
   156 | }
